2025-06-18T11:46:33.8430969Z <pr_title>
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8431725Z Simplify CI: consolidate bloated workflows and add immediate wheel access
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8432652Z </pr_title>
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8432908Z 
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8433109Z <pr_description>
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8434399Z This PR addresses the CI complexity and reliability issues by dramatically simplifying the workflow structure and providing multiple access methods for package installation.
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8435440Z 
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8435552Z ## Problem
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8435989Z The repository had **6 CI workflow files** with significant redundancy:
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8436559Z - `build-artifacts.yaml`
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8437131Z - `build-packages-dev.yaml` 
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8437848Z - `build-packages-prod.yaml`
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8438438Z - `build-packages-staging.yaml`
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8438980Z - `create-release.yaml`
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8439283Z - `tools.yaml`
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8439439Z 
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8440186Z This created maintenance complexity and CI reliability issues, making it difficult to successfully build and access wheel artifacts.
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8440914Z 
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8441023Z ## Solution
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8441167Z 
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8441290Z ### 1. Consolidated CI Workflows
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8441713Z **Reduced from 6 to 3 focused workflows** (50% reduction):
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8442049Z 
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8442555Z - **`build-and-deploy.yaml`**: Unified workflow that builds once and deploys to appropriate environments based on triggers
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8443664Z - **`commit-wheels.yaml`**: Fallback workflow that commits wheels directly to the repository when CI builds succeed
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8444532Z - **`tools.yaml`**: Unchanged, handles build tools and container management
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8444927Z 
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8445058Z ### 2. Multiple Installation Methods
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8445432Z Users can now install packages via:
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8445673Z 
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8445792Z **One-line installer:**
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8446081Z ```bash
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8446739Z curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/install.sh | bash -s -- pandas
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8447292Z ```
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8447629Z 
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8447726Z **Direct wheel download:**
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8447959Z ```bash
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8448604Z curl -L https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/wheels/pandas-1.5.0-cp310-cp310-linux_armv7l.whl -o /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8449338Z pip install /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8449567Z ```
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8449662Z 
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8449819Z **CI artifacts and releases** (when CI runs successfully)
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8450064Z 
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8450164Z ### 3. Immediate Package Access
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8450750Z Added pre-built wheel files directly to the repository in the `wheels/` directory, so packages are immediately available even if CI fails.
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8451270Z 
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8451348Z ## Key Benefits
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8451603Z - **Simplified maintenance**: 50% fewer workflow files
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8452097Z - **Eliminated redundancy**: Single build logic instead of duplicated across multiple files
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8452634Z - **Improved reliability**: Multiple fallback access methods
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8453133Z - **Better user experience**: Immediate access to packages via curl/pip as requested
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8453710Z - **Clear documentation**: Added `CI-SIMPLIFICATION.md` explaining the changes
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8454025Z 
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8454404Z The solution directly addresses the "bloated" CI concern while ensuring reliable package access through multiple channels.
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8454881Z 
2025-06-18T11:46:33.8454965Z Fixes #11.

2025-06-18T11:46:51.9873956Z     109.## Key Components Explained
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9874210Z     110.
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9874401Z     111.### 1. Pre-built ARM Wheels
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9874642Z     112.
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9875030Z     113.The installation uses specially built ARMv7l-compatible wheels:
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9875410Z     114.
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9875762Z     115.- **pendulum-3.1.0**: Solves Rust compilation issues that blocked Prefect installation
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9876292Z     116.- **ujson-5.10.0**: Custom fallback wheel for JSON processing on ARM
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9876767Z     117.- **prefect-3.3.4**: Full Prefect package with flow/task decorator support
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9877139Z     118.
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9877709Z     119.> **Build Instructions**: For details on how these wheels were built, see `WHEEL-BUILD-INSTRUCTIONS.md`
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9878199Z     120.
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9878407Z     121.### 2. Environment Configuration
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9878666Z     122.
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9878934Z     123.The OT-2 requires specific PATH and PYTHONPATH settings:
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9879355Z     124.- Packages install to `/var/user-packages/root/.local/`
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9879711Z     125.- CLI tools need to be added to PATH
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9880036Z     126.- Python modules need PYTHONPATH configuration
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9880334Z     127.
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9880527Z     128.### 3. Dependency Resolution
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9880777Z     129.
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9881363Z     130.Force-reinstalling core wheels with `--no-deps` prevents pip from attempting to compile dependencies that fail on the OT-2's limited environment.
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9882006Z     131.
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9882190Z     132.## Usage Examples
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9882410Z     133.

2025-06-18T11:46:51.9882980Z     135.
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9883154Z     136.```python
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9883372Z     137.from prefect import flow, task
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9883629Z     138.
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9883798Z     139.@task
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9884109Z     140.def prepare_protocol():

2025-06-18T11:46:51.9884648Z     142.    return "ready"
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9884865Z     143.
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9885036Z     144.@task  
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9885242Z     145.def run_experiment(status):
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9885566Z     146.    print(f"Running experiment with status: {status}")

2025-06-18T11:46:51.9886130Z     148.
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9886304Z     149.@flow

2025-06-18T11:46:51.9887030Z     152.    result = run_experiment(status)
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9887506Z     153.    print(f"Workflow finished: {result}")

2025-06-18T11:46:51.9888316Z     156.# Run the workflow
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9888547Z     157.if __name__ == "__main__":
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9888803Z     158.    ot2_workflow()
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9889021Z     159.```
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9889197Z     160.

2025-06-18T11:46:51.9912624Z     255.After successful installation, you can:
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9912903Z     256.
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9913315Z     257.✅ **Create and run Prefect flows** with `@flow` and `@task` decorators  
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9913841Z     258.✅ **Serve flows** using `flow.serve()` for remote execution  
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9914275Z     259.✅ **Use Prefect CLI** for workflow management

2025-06-18T11:46:51.9915884Z     263.## Architecture Notes
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9916113Z     264.
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9916382Z     265.- **Environment**: Debian-based OT-2 firmware with Python 3.10
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9916758Z     266.- **Architecture**: ARMv7l (32-bit ARM)
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9917128Z     267.- **Package Location**: `/var/user-packages/root/.local/`
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9917774Z     268.- **Shell**: busybox ash (limited bash features)
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9918075Z     269.
2025-06-18T11:46:51.9918604Z     270.This installation process resolves all compilation issues that prevent standard pip installation of Prefect on OT-2 hardware.

2025-06-18T11:46:59.3170919Z     7.The three essential wheels are:

2025-06-18T11:46:59.3173123Z     10.- `prefect-3.3.4-py3-none-any.whl` - Prefect workflow engine (pure Python)

2025-06-18T11:46:59.3174200Z     12.## Prerequisites

2025-06-18T11:46:59.3233382Z     115.
